## Combo 3609-6881--127
Cards: Moxite Refinery + Magistrate's Scepter
Color identity: C | Bracket: Ruthless | Popularity: 7851 | Commander: legal | Mana needed: {2} each turn
Produces: Infinite turns; Lock
Requires: Spacecraft (battlefield)
Start: Moxite Refinery (battlefield); Magistrate's Scepter (battlefield)
Prerequisites: Other creatures you control have power 3 or greater.
Steps: Activate a Spacecraft's station ability by tapping a creature you control, putting a number of charge counters on the Spacecraft equal to the creature's power.
  If necessary, repeat step 1 until the Spacecraft has three or more charge counters on it.
  Activate Moxite Refinery by paying {2}, tapping it and removing three charge counters from the Spacecraft, putting three charge counters on Magistrate's Scepter.
  Activate Magistrate's Scepter's second ability by tapping it and removing three charge counters from it, causing you to take an extra turn after this one.
  Repeat each turn.
Notes: You can move as many charge counters from the Spacecraft to Magistrate's Scepter. If you move more than three, you may be able to forego steps 1-3 during some turns.

## Combo 977-1020-1766-2866-4659
Cards: Scrap Trawler + Krark-Clan Ironworks + Myr Retriever + Chromatic Star + Thran Dynamo

Start: Scrap Trawler (battlefield); Krark-Clan Ironworks (battlefield); Myr Retriever (battlefield); Chromatic Star (battlefield); Thran Dynamo (battlefield)
Steps: Announce the activation of Chromatic Star's second ability.
  During this activation, activate Krark-Clan Ironworks twice by sacrificing Thran Dynamo and Myr Retriever, adding {C}{C}{C}{C}.
  Continue activating and resolving the Chromatic Star ability, paying {1}, tapping it, and sacrificing it, adding {1}.
  Scrap Trawler triggers twice, Myr Retriever triggers once, and Chromatic Star triggers once.
  Resolve the Chromatic Star trigger, drawing a card.
  Resolve the Scrap Trawler trigger from Myr Retriever, returning Chromatic Star from your graveyard to your hand.
  Resolve the Scrap Trawler trigger from Thran Dynamo, returning Myr Retriever from your graveyard to your hand.
  Resolve the Myr Retriever trigger, returning Thran Dynamo from your graveyard to your hand.
  Cast Chromatic Star by paying {1}.
  Cast Myr Retriever by paying {2}.
  Cast Thran Dynamo by paying {4}.
  Repeat.
Notes: This combo works by taking advantage of the fact that you can activate mana abilities during the declared activation of an activated ability. This allows you to delay putting the Trawler and Retriever triggers on the stack (and selecting their targets) until all permanents you need to target are in the graveyard. For more information, please see rules 605.3, 602.2b, and 601.2g.

Color identity: C | Bracket: Exhibition | Popularity: 10 | Commander: legal
Produces: Infinite creature LTB; Infinite creature ETB; Infinite colored mana; Infinite creature sacrifice triggers; Infinite death triggers
Requires: Creature with Bloodthirst (battlefield)
Start: Cauldron of Souls (battlefield); Dross Scorpion (battlefield); Phyrexian Altar (battlefield); Mycosynth Lattice (battlefield)
Prerequisites: An opponent was dealt damage this turn.
  The creature with bloodthirst does not have a -1/-1 counter on it.
Steps: Activate Cauldron of Souls by tapping it, giving the creature with bloodthirst persist until end of turn.
  Activate Phyrexian Altar by sacrificing the creature with bloodthirst, adding one mana of any color.
  When the creature dies, its persist ability and Dross Scorpion trigger.
  Resolve the persist trigger, returning the creature from your graveyard to the battlefield, which enters the battlefield with at least one +1/+1 counter on it due to its bloodthirst ability.
  Resolve the Dross Scorpion trigger, untapping Cauldron of Souls.
  Repeat.

  Cast Krak-Clan Ironworks, Myr Retriver, Chromatic Star, and Mana Crypt by paying seven mana.
  Repeat.
Notes: The purpose the delay mentioned in step 1 is to wait until all relevant artifacts are in your graveyard before you have to select targets for triggered abilities.
